# Selecta ledger
Every task lives here with an ID. Each reply names IDs ticked since the last reply and reports `done/total`. Nothing is started that is not here; nothing is reported done that is not ticked. Plans: `thoughts/shared/plans/` (status in each file's frontmatter; see RULES.md rule 0).
Session 2026-10-22 (1). Finish line: design doc complete (sources, feature bar, tiers, view styles, playlist model, tag ABI, decisions), research docs written, ledger restructured, first commit made, next-session prompt written. Done.
Session 2026-10-23 (3). Finish line: M1.1 plan approved, rows in this ledger (R8, R9, L3).
Session 2026-10-22 (2). Finish line: RULES.md rule 0 (plans and ledger never stale), M0a plan written and approved, ledger rows I1 to I22 added, implementation started.

| I124 | [ ] | 4 | AHI sink over ahi.device | rig sentinel `ahi_bufs=`; rig-only: AHI sink not started, needs rig (Q49) FS-UAE 2026-10-24 (32b09a8): rig has ahi.device 4.180 and AudioModes, but no AHI sink exists (no plugins/outputs/ahi, no ahi_bufs). Sink must be written; then verify on FS-UAE. Host 2026-10-24 (afa72e4): plugins/outputs/ahi sink (CMD_WRITE double buffer, ahi_bufs/underruns stats) + ahi-shadow; ahi-unit, ahi-routing desktop+vamos green. OPEN: FS-UAE run `render --output ahi` on 68020 must print ahi_rate=28150 ahi_buffer_frames=4096 underruns=0. FS-UAE f175950: plugins list ahi, render and play return rc=0, ahi_bufs=13 matches 56300 frames mixed. Underruns=12 because cycle-exact 68020 decodes ~33x slower than realtime; underruns=0 needs a realtime run (Replay or lighter replayer). play prints no output= stats line, so play counters unchecked. Stays open. Replay 2026-10-25 (main 0e98459, UpRough:selecta-test/, Stack 200000): `render --output ahi --seconds 10 selecta-test.mod` FAIL: `output=ahi ahi_bufs=430 underruns=426 ahi_buffer_frames=1024 ahi_rate=44100` (mixed_frames=441000). Wrong profile (44100/1024, not 28150/4096) and 426/430 buffers late; wasabid stopped answering ~25 s during the run, so something starves other tasks. Next: Amiga agent hunts why the 68020 profile was not picked, decode speed vs realtime, and the task priority/busy wait. Replay 2026-10-25 (agent, findings in session scratchpad i124-findings.md): sink and tier are right; the 68060 class gets 44100/1024 by design (ahi_cfg.c), 28150/4096 is the 68020 class only. Underruns come from decode slower than realtime on the 060: 10 s of selecta-test.mod takes 23.9 s at 44100 with --output null (0.42x), 15.6 s at 28150 (0.64x); resampler is about 13% of it. Sink uses WaitIO (65 ms waited in 27.8 s), no busy wait. NEXT: profile which replayer claims .mod on the Amiga and its cost (nuade vs kplayer vs openmpt). Diagnostic pump_ms/STAT_AHI_DECODE_MS/STAT_AHI_WAIT_MS patch is uncommitted in render.c, tags.def, ahi_out.c. | Replay 060 2026-10-25 ($S/i124-replayer.md): the 0.42x decode was nuade on selecta-test.mod, which has no M.K. magic so kplayer rejects it; M.K. files go to kplayer on the Paula hardware route (no CPU mixing, realtime). Underruns apply only to modules kplayer cannot claim (15-sample, non-M.K. magics). Retest with an M.K. module; selection order bug logged as I293.

| I217 | [x] | - | Selecta raised recoverable alert #00080000 (AG_BadParm) at every start on the Replay 68060 (KS 47.111), even on `--output bogus`. Cause: `ahi_present()` in `src/core/amiga/machine.c` opened `ahi.device` with a 48-byte IOStdReq; AHI needs an 80-byte AHIRequest with `ahir_Version` at offset 48. Measured with a probe on the Replay: 48 bytes gives open -1 plus the alert, 80 bytes with version 4 gives open 0 and no alert. The probe therefore also reported no AHI on real hardware. Fix: `ahi_request_create` allocates 80 bytes and sets the version; sentinel `sel_machine_ahi_request` prints `ahi.request=80/4` in `selecta-cli machine`; T50 vamos test checks it (fails with 48/4 when the size is reverted). Verified on the Replay: no alert, `AUDIO=paula,ahi`. |
| I218 | [x] | - | Arrow keys did nothing in Selecta on the Replay; other keys worked (Johan, 2026-10-24). Cause: the Amiga console.device sends keys after the 8-bit CSI 0x9B (Up is 9B 41), and `plugins/ui/text/keys.c` only knew ESC [, so 0x9B became U+FFFD and the final byte a letter. Fix: 0x9B starts a CSI in the Amiga console dialect: arrows, Shift+arrows (T, S, space A, space @), F1..F10 (0~..9~, Shift 10~..19~), PC keys (Insert 40~, Page Up 41~, Page Down 42~, Home 44~, End 45~, Shift 50~..55~); Help (?~) is dropped. Tests: grid-unit decoder cases; amiga-console-keys-vamos drives the Amiga Selecta with the raw bytes (view_cursor==1); both fail with the fix reverted. Johan confirmed it works through UP-Term (2026-10-24); the native Replay keyboard and console path is still to confirm. |
| I218a | [x] | - | Amiga console text input is ISO-8859-1 (a-umlaut arrives as one byte 0xE4), but `keys.c` decodes bytes above 0x7F as UTF-8, so a non-ASCII letter typed on the Amiga swallows the next bytes. Decide the Amiga input charset path (Latin-1 to code point when the console is the Amiga console) and test it through the Amiga Selecta. Also: Del (0x7F) and Backspace (0x08) both map to Backspace on the Amiga console. Done 2026-10-24: each terminal backend declares txt_term_amiga_console; the key decoder then reads Latin-1, 0x08 Backspace and 0x7F Del, and the grid flushes Latin-1 (? above U+00FF). grid-unit and amiga-console-keys-vamos (view_cursor==3 after a 0xE4 byte) fail with the fix reverted. |
| I218b | [x] | - | F-keys and Shift+arrows did nothing on the Replay keyboard (Johan, 2026-10-24): the I218 decoder delivered them, but no default binds them and key names had no Shift prefix, so keys.conf could not bind Shift either. Johan's decisions: add an S- prefix, an unbound Shift+key acts as the plain key, and the F-keys stay free for keys.conf (no default layout). Fix in `plugins/ui/text/keybind.c`: S- parse and name, and `txt_bindings_lookup` falls back to the key without Shift. Tests: keybind-unit (S- names, fallback, a bound S- row wins); amiga-console-keys-vamos sends Shift+Down as 0x9B S and needs view_cursor==2. Both fail with the fallback reverted. Johan to confirm Shift+arrows on the Replay keyboard. |
| I218c | [x] | - | Johan 2026-10-24 on the Replay: Selecta does not fill the Shell window and does not redraw when the window is resized. Cause: plugins/ui/text/amiga/term.c asks the size once at open with xterm CSI 18 t, which the native console never answers, so it stays 80x24. Fix: ask with the console Window Status Request (CSI 0 SPACE q, reply CSI 1;1;rows;cols SPACE r) as well, enable raw event class 12 (window resized, CSI 12 {) when the native reply came, parse both reports in keys.c, re-ask on a resize event and feed the new size to txt_term_size so the existing resize path in text_ui.c redraws. | test: keys unit test for both reports; flow test through the text UI showing a resize event and bounds report regrow the grid; Johan confirms full window and redraw on resize on the Replay

## Open decisions
Round 5 picks decided 2026-10-22 (AskUserQuestion, two calls): in = F19, F20, F21, F23, F24, F25, F29, F30, F31, F33 to F41, F43 to F46, F48, F49; restored into scope = F50 multi-zone sync, F51 server transcoding, F52 multi-user/multi-library; not picked = F32 CD audio, F42 rippers, F47 device sync; still out = Atmos, mpd client messaging, Tidal/Qobuz. Recorded in design doc sections 1a and 16. Open: none.
- [x] D-M2-1 (default taken, Q35, plan 2026-10-24-m2-amiga.md): nuade's voice API is float/double, so a nuade player ticking under a CIA interrupt on a 68020 without FPU runs soft-float in the interrupt (rule 4). Options for M2: tick in a high-priority task, upstream an integer voice API to emoon/nuade, or require an FPU for Paula-direct nuade. Source: thoughts/shared/research/2026-10-23_nuade-adapter.md. Not blocking M0b.
- [x] D-M2-2 (default taken, Q36): plain M.K. .mod on the Amiga. No nuade player probes M.K. (R8) and libopenmpt stays desktop-only in M1.1. Options for M2: a nuade M.K. player upstream (author is a friend of the user) or the UADE-native plugin. Decide at M2 planning.
- [x] D-M4-1 (Johan, 2026-10-24, AskUserQuestion): M3 finishes first (I202 green, I203 confirmed), then the M4 plan is written. No M4 code before that.
- [x] D-M4-2 (Johan, 2026-10-24): M4 leads with the most modern Amiga UI possible, built to modern UI standards from the references in the design doc (modern players are the bar), and the other Amiga UIs (MUI, GadTools, ReAction) are downscaled from it afterwards. M4 adds a new UI plugin not in the design doc: a hardware-banging custom UI, one variant for AGA (direct chipset, copper/blitter, maximum speed and fast graphics content) and one for RTG. Total custom, no toolkit limits. The M4 plan must add it to design doc sections 1a and 14.
- [x] D-M4-3 (Johan, 2026-10-24): every Amiga UI, Workbench window UIs included, opens using the full screen area by default (modern players are big; use all available screen space). Each UI also offers switchable compact modes for when Selecta should not cover the whole screen. The M4 plan specifies the compact modes and how to switch between them. The text UI already fills the window it gets once I218c lands.
- [x] D-M4-4 (Johan, 2026-10-24, AskUserQuestion): amends D-M4-1. The M4 Amiga UI plan may be written now, in parallel with the end of M3; still no M4 code before M3 finishes (I202 green, I203 confirmed). Plan file: thoughts/shared/plans/2026-10-24-m4-amiga-ui.md.
- [x] D-M4-5 (Johan, 2026-10-24, AskUserQuestion): the Mac desktop UI (SDL2, design D5) is built before the custom Amiga UI, in a new milestone after M3 (moved out of M5), so both UIs look the same. M4 toolkit UIs (MUI, ReAction, GadTools) do not wait for it; the custom Amiga UI does.
- [x] D-M4-6 (Johan, 2026-10-24, AskUserQuestion): one widget set, layout and token layer in C with three backends: SDL (desktop), RTG and AGA (Amiga). Same look by construction. Design direction: modern streaming-app look, Spotify as the reference; Johan rejected the Chipmachine two-screen layout as "cheesy retro" (M4 plan Q20). Other M4 plan answers are in its section 8a. Open: revise M4 plan sections 3, 4, 6 and the design doc milestones (section 14) to match.
- [x] D-I199-1 (Johan, 2026-10-26, AskUserQuestion): the I199 rig assertion reads the existing `fetches=` and `mirror_tries=` counters from an Execute-script redirect of `--stats` to RAM: (fetches=1, mirror_tries>=1). No new mirror_fetches counter, no core change. The mirror route is reached with SELECTA_DEVILBOX_URL=http://devilbox.uprough.net, SELECTA_NET_MAP=devilbox.uprough.net=http://127.0.0.1:<port>, SELECTA_NET_ALLOW=127.0.0.1.
Protocol additions from M1d are already in design doc section 3 (not a decision, engine protocol detail).

## Facts gathered
- 2026-10-23 DEViLBOX recheck (repo ~/Code/DEViLBOX, live https://devilbox.uprough.net/api). No API key exists anywhere; auth is a JWT. POST /api/auth/login {username,password} -> {token,user} (server/src/routes/auth.ts:83), JWT 30 days, sent as `Authorization: Bearer` (server/src/middleware). Live: login answers 400 on an empty body, so the route is deployed. Unblocks I173 under D-M3-12 as written.
- Hip vote exists since DEViLBOX 704a7b4eb (server/src/routes/hip.ts): GET /api/hip/vote?k=<32 hex device token>&m=<12 hex key>&r=0|5[&n=<name><=255>][&p=<player><=64>] and GET /api/hip/votes?m=&k=; reply text/plain "<count> <yours>\n", errors "ERR bad\n" (400), "ERR rate\n" (429). m is the audacious-uade xxhash32 key (HippoPlayer-68k thoughts/spot/voting-proposal.md:5,85). Token: random 128-bit, created on first vote, kept in prefs. Live: votes?m=a5cbd2fd4dbe answers "0 0". Answers Q69; unblocks I139a and I174.
- Ratings PUT accepts source modland or hvsc only (server/src/routes/ratings.ts:49); local songs have no remote rating.
- Loudness: no loudness endpoint. /api/analysis/lookup/<sha256> (server/src/routes/analysis.ts:23) returns DJ analysis with rmsDb, peakDb and an `extended` loudness set; POST /api/analysis/cache requires bpm > 0, so Selecta cannot write it.
- Share links: DEViLBOX web app reads only ?view= (src/App.tsx:114), ?collab=, ?room=, ?host=. No ?song= or ?playlist= deep link exists; Selecta's I180 song and playlist links open nothing in DEViLBOX today.
- nuade: C port of uade replayers, MIT, CMake target nuade::nuade. Tick players (framework owns clock + mixer) and self-rendering players (render_raw float stereo). Display hooks: position, voice_info, events, tracker_info/cells. Headers: scratchpad ext/nuade/include/nuade/*.h.
- bbmp: Python + Textual TUI; replayers are sandboxed helper processes in csrc/ plus libopenmpt/gme/sidplayfp/uade; docs/replayers.md, docs/collections.md, docs/formats.md.
- DEViLBOX API: base https://devilbox.uprough.net/api; ratings 1-5 (PUT/DELETE /api/ratings, Bearer JWT); batch GET max 200; item key = source + Modland full_path / HVSC path; genre GET /api/genre/:key (xxh32 of first 256 KB + size & 0xFFFF); songdb lookup by hash; modland and hvsc browse/search/download. No genre-list or artist endpoint.
- uprough-uade: UADE score.s host + real Eagleplayer/DeliTracker 68k binaries natively, Paula direct, no mixing; asm contract _music_init/start/stop/shutdown/play1/play2/set_pos/select; 40 formats on A500.
- HippoPlayer source: /Users/spot/Code/HippoPlayer-68k/code/puu016.s (playlist load 13119, header 13523, save 13542).
Note 2026-10-23: the handoff's uninvestigated failures paula14-tables, paula14-tables-vamos, registry-machine-filter-vamos, rp-config-taglist-vamos and paula-voice-registers-vamos all pass on main at 6b9d32a. Only amiga-text-ui-vamos (T55 ui_hash) remains open from that list.

| I282 | [x] | I278 | Johan 2026-10-25 (Mac): "the pattern data is static it doesnt roll". Root cause: nuade never exported RP_POSITION and the engine sampled the position only after rp_render, never in the rp_tick (voice replayer) loop, so the Tracker page row froze at the last SEEK. | I290 | [x] | I220f | Johan 2026-10-25 (Mac): screenshot shows "OS4 [box] OS2 [box] ... UNPARTY [box] ..." in Now Playing. Root cause found: libopenmpt returns a MOD's sample names as the song message, joined with '\n' (electrobeats.mod samples OS4, OS2, OS5, OS7, OS6, UNPARTY). lyrics_take (src/engine/engine.c ~4877) keeps it as lyr_plain; desktop_ui.c ~3747 draws st->lyric_text with single-line draw_text, so each '\n' hits the glyph lookup and renders the missing-glyph box. Invariant: control characters never reach a glyph; multi-line text is laid out as lines. | I348 | [x] | I347 | Amiga, rig 2: `--exec "LIBRARY_SCAN SYS:selecta-test/mods"` adds nothing (songs=0, scans=0, frames=1, no error, empty log; 4 mods of 6166 B present, RAM:lib.db created 131072 B). Agent hunts the root cause (cmd_LIBRARY_SCAN, scan_task, Amiga job path without pthreads, SYS: path handling, opendir/readdir on bebbo gcc, extension sniffing), fixes it with a regression test under tests/, proves songs>=4 and that Down changes the frame, leaves Selecta running populated for Johan's look. Report $S/rig2/i348-report.md. Finding 2026-10-26 (agent, no code change): the scan works on Amiga (`scan_task ok=1 files=4 dirs=1`, `stat Songs in library|4` via `--exec STATS_SHOW`; the Amiga build runs the scan inline, src/library/db/job.c:178). `songs=0` is the UI search counter (desktop_ui.c:2873), not the library count. Real bug in desktop_ui.c: LIBRARY_RESULT is handled only while `scan_pending` is set (:6169, set :5389 when the UI sends the scan itself); a boot-time `--exec LIBRARY_SCAN` finishes before the UI loop, so the page never reloads. Re-pointed: Mac track fix = reload on LIBRARY_RESULT regardless of scan_pending, plus focusable rows so one Down key changes the frame on `--view library` (focus_moves=0 today, Songs page lists playlists). Host test: `--exec LIBRARY_SCAN <dir>` then `--stats` shows the rows loaded. frames=1 is by design (event-driven). Rig 2 shows Library with 4 songs. 2026-10-26 UI agent report (no commit, tree reverted, WIP at scratchpad i348ui/wip.patch + scan_exec.cmake): premise correction: with --exec the scan runs BEFORE the window opens so the page loads after it (old code shows Songs in library|3); the real symptom needs a scan sent while the window is up (test dui-scan-exec: input script wait 1 / exec LIBRARY_SCAN dir / wait 4, red at scan_exec.cmake:31). An unconditional reload on LIBRARY_RESULT is WRONG: LIBRARY_RESULT also answers LIBRARY_SEARCH and browse with the same SRC_PATH/SRC_COUNT tags, the handler sends LIBRARY_SEARCH itself so it feeds back; breaks dui-library (library.cmake:137), dui-nowplaying, dui-hover-sidebar-ink. Chosen fix (next agent): engine-side library-changed serial (same pattern as panel_serial, I288) bumped where a scan, sync or fetch finishes (emit_library_path callers src/engine/engine.c ~3356, ~4518, ~4544), exposed in the state; the UI reloads the page when the serial changes. Second real bug in the patch: headless loop (desktop_ui.c ~6375) keeps turning only while scan_pending, so a scan from another sender is never waited for; fix desk_scan_active() = sel_engine_scan_progress().active in that wait condition (green in wip.patch). Part 2 (focusable rows) premise wrong: focus_moves counts only Tab, Down sends VIEW_CURSOR +1 (arrows=1), engine behaviour same on Mac and Amiga. Open product question: on --view library the default Composers tab lists only songs with a composer tag; test mods have none, so no rows; fall back to another tab or show an Unknown group (ask Johan). 2026-10-26 part 1 DONE (commit below): engine sel_state.library_serial (bumped in emit_library_path, the single helper of the 3 callers scan/sync/fetch at engine.c ~3356, ~4518, ~4544), desktop_ui.c desk_library_follow reloads the page when it moves (stats `library_serial=N library_reloads=M`), headless loop also turns while desk_scan_active(); plan DUI-P3-14, test dui-library-reload (red on HEAD: `no library_serial line`; green: serial>=1, reloads>=1, Songs in library|3). Part 2 dropped (premise wrong); the empty Composers tab is I353. |

Weight per the table: 0 at the frame presented in the motion turn, 29% at 16 ms, 50% at 29 ms, 90% at 68 ms, full at 100 ms; sweeping down a list restarts at 0 per row so no row reaches full while the pointer moves. Second, Home-only cause: a0fd320 redrew the whole Home per hover change, fixed in bdc4dff (DUI-P3-8); needs the live window relaunched on the new binary. | I374 | [x] | I371 | Mac toasts do not animate and stay on screen (Johan 2026-10-26: "the toasts dont animate and they get stuck"; "they should probably use a gradient as bg matching the rest of the ui"). Cause (read from desktop_ui.c): the live SDL loop (~6628) asks only desk_search_wait/desk_search_tick for its poll timeout; desk_toast_wait/desk_toast_tick (and desk_tip_wait/desk_tip_tick) are called only from desk_advance (~4810), the test clock path, so in the live window a toast clears only when some later event wakes the loop past toast_until. The toast has no tween at all. Fix: one shared timer step (wait = min of tip, search, toast; then tick all three) called by both desk_advance and the live loop, so the clock paths cannot drift again; toast enters and leaves with an ANIM_KEY_TOAST tween (slide up plus fade, FAST_MS class, ease out), and its background is a vertical sel_gui_gradient like the Home tint (~1216) from ACCENT toward a darker accent mix, rounded, label ACCENT_TEXT. | tests/dui: live-loop case (SELECTA_SDL_TEST_LIVE=1, timed) that triggers a toast (Add to playlist via song menu), then idles with no input past TOAST_MS: --stats toast_on=0 and a frame dump shows the toast rect back to main colour; a mid-tween frame shows partial offset; fails on e297c29. Label song-menu or another non-dui label (ratchet). | Same file as I372/I373: Mac slot, after I372, fold with I373. Check whether tips stick the same way in the live window and fix in the same shared step. Done 7285660; Johan to check by eye on Mac. |

| I526 | [ ] | I468 | Text on the 640x256 AGA desktop is too small to read: Johan, 2026-10-27: "the texts are too small to be really readable". The layout is drawn at ui_logical 1024x819 and scaled down to the screen, so baked glyphs shrink below a readable size. Text must be readable at its native pixel size on 640x256 (font sizes chosen for the screen, layout fitting a smaller logical size), not scaled down. Decision (Johan, 2026-10-27): 640x256 screens use bitmap fonts; the font is a bundled pixel font baked into the binary (2-3 sizes, drawn for the 2:1 tall pixels, like topaz 8), not the system font via diskfont. Text is drawn at native screen pixels, so the layout must lay out at 640x256 natively instead of drawing at 1024x819 and scaling down. Fonts (Johan, 2026-10-27: "one 8x8 and one mini", "hippoplayer has a minifont bundled that we could mimic"): two bundled bitmap fonts, an 8x8 for normal text and a mini font for dense rows, the mini drawn in the style of HippoPlayer's mini font. That font is mini4.font (sizes 6 and 8, loaded through OpenDiskFont, see HippoPlayer-68k code/puu016.s mini_text_attr; copies in HippoPlayer-68k/a1200-test/Fonts/mini4). It is a separate disk font, not covered by HippoPlayer's MIT licence, so we draw our own glyphs in its style instead of copying its bitmaps. Reference set from Johan, 2026-10-27: /Volumes/home/fonts holds mini4 (4x6, 4x8), mini6 (6x6, 6x8) and mini8 (8x8), all fixed width, chars 32-255, FPF_DESIGNED, no author or licence string in the files. Decision (Johan, 2026-10-27): ship these bitmaps as they are ("old public domain fonts ... it's fine"); this replaces the earlier draw-our-own note. Use all three families, mini4, mini6 and mini8, baked into the binary and loaded at native pixels; which family each UI role uses is part of the I526 plan. Headlines (Johan, 2026-10-27, for the 640x256 UI: "we need bitmap fonts for the headlines as well... fonts of varying sizes"): two headline sizes, a page title of about 16 rows and a section heading of about 11 rows, baked at build time from Inter SemiBold (the Mac desktop font, plugins/ui/desktop/CMakeLists.txt, gui_fontbake) at native 640x256 pixels with 2:1 tall-pixel correction and antialiased edges drawn from index8 palette shades. Body and list text use mini8, mini6 and mini4. | Johan reads every label on Home and Library on rig 2 without effort | Plan approved 2026-10-27 (thoughts/shared/plans/2026-10-27-aga-native-fonts.md, D1-D6 as recommended, 3b15886). P1-P2b done on branch worktree-agent-aef30ca469ddcc5ed (dde8531, report thoughts/shared/reports/i526-p1-p2b-report.md). P3-P5 agent started 2026-10-27 in the same worktree, host tests only; report i526-p3-p5-report.md. Rig 2 check after I532 frees the rig. Decision (Johan, 2026-10-27): no preview of the P1-P2b branch; Johan checks the fonts on rig 2 once P3-P5 is done, which starts after I531/I532. |
| I527 | [~] | I265 | Sync on launch (Johan 2026-10-27): a remote source never synced, or not synced for N days (library.sync_stale_days, default 30, 0 = off), shows a non-blocking banner at the top of the desktop Library page: checkbox per due source (default ticked), Sync and Not now. Sync sends LIBRARY_SYNC per ticked source (Sources page path); Not now persists library.sync_notice_at. | dui-sync-banner (real clicks, sentinel, revert-checked), source-sync-due unit test, sources and sources-off expected files | Done 2026-10-27 (bcbdad0), cherry-picked onto main; awaiting Johan live check on the Mac Library page. Johan, 2026-10-27: "mac selecta still gets spinning umbrella when i press sync". Cause (sample of the hung process): cmd_LIBRARY_SYNC (src/engine/engine.c) runs src->sync inline in sel_engine_pump on the main thread; the ZXArt sync blocks in sel_zxart_net_sync > sel_sync_net_get_file > sel_http_request > sel_net_recv and sel_sync_net_pause. Fix: run LIBRARY_SYNC as a library task like LIBRARY_SCAN (sel_library_task_start, collect in the pump, progress, queue), not inline. Fix (2026-10-27): LIBRARY_SYNC runs as a library task like the scan (sel_library_task_start, collected in the pump), queued behind a scan or sync, one queue slot per source (SEL_SYNC_QUEUE = SEL_SOURCE_MAX) so the sync-all banner never loses a source; see thoughts/shared/reports/i527-sync-async-report.md, commits 2c9eda9 and dbb3662. Open: quitting during a network sync blocks in sel_engine_destroy until the net timeout (sync does not poll cancel). Open: pattern-cells and home-view fail in this worktree, not touched by this change, unverified on main; see thoughts/shared/reports/i527-sync-on-launch-report.md. |
| I528 | [~] | - | The Mac desktop freezes after Sync with a dark empty bar in the bottom right: Johan, 2026-10-27: "i get a black bar in the bottom corner when i press sync it's empty i guess its supposed to be a progressbar". The bar is the 'Syncing <name>' toast stuck at tween 0 (fill and ink both equal SURFACE) because the UI thread is blocked. Process sample: main > send_line > sel_engine_pump > cmd_COMPOSERS_SHOW > sel_library_composer_list > sel_genre_call > pthread_cond_wait, while the library worker runs sqlite3_step. Root cause 1 (library layer): sel_library_composer_list (src/library/db/songdb.c:389) fetches one composer per request, and op_composer_list (songdb.c ~181) re-runs the full GROUP BY over song_composers with LIMIT 1 OFFSET n each time; about 3.0 s per row on the live DB (516,675 songs, 21,817 composers), so quadratic, hours in total. Fix: one pass (one query streamed through the sink, or keyset paging), not per-row OFFSET. Root cause 2 (engine): engine commands run on the UI thread and wait synchronously on library requests; check design doc section on the library thread for whether such list queries must be async. Also check user.c:411 and similar.c:70 for the same pattern. | Johan presses Sync on the Mac desktop with the live library and the UI stays responsive; the Composers page fills in under a second || Root cause 1 fixed 2026-10-27 (c90a5d0, on main as cc4171e): composer list, similar, also-by and duplicates stream all rows through one library request; regression test library-scan-composers asserts one request for 300 composers (revert-checked). Live DB copy: composer list from about 18.5 h (3.05 s per row) to 1.9 s. Open: 1.9 s is still above the "under a second" acceptance and still blocks the UI thread; the same GROUP BY returns SQLITE_NOMEM on an Amiga-sized heap. Needs a design decision (maintained composer table or async list). Report thoughts/shared/reports/i528-composer-list-report.md. Decision (Johan, 2026-10-27): maintained composer table with song counts, kept current by the composer write paths, so the list is a plain read on Mac and Amiga. Follow-up 2026-10-27 (3712003): cc4171e ran the row callbacks on the library worker, so radio deadlocked (admit calls sel_library_flag_skipped from a similar/also-by row; ctest radio timed out at 60 s). sel_genre_call now buffers rows on the worker and replays them on the caller thread after the reply; radio passes in 1.9 s, times out without the fix. Composer table (v7: composers, composer_spellings, composer_unknown, 6 triggers); list is a plain read, 6.4 ms host / 6.5 ms a060 on 21,818 rows; migration 2.65 s on 516,675 songs; report thoughts/shared/reports/i528-composer-table-report.md. |

Bug 2 cause: the Amiga build compiles with SEL_NO_TLS (src/library/CMakeLists.txt:37-38), so tls.c returns SEL_TLS_E_UNAVAILABLE for every https open and https://modland.com/ fails at once. Correction (Johan, 2026-10-27): the plans already settle this. The Amiga fetches every online source over plain http through the DEViLBOX mirror proxy /api/mirror/<source>/<path> (online-sources plan line 68, design D9, I156 client route mirrors.c:80 amiga_route). The server side lives in ~/Code/hippoplayer.se (plan 2026-10-26-mirror-endpoint.md): code phases P1-P6 are done, and the rollout is paused because hippoplayer.se is down (Johan, 2026-10-27). Probes 2026-10-27: devilbox.uprough.net/api/mirror/modland/ answers 404; its http answers 308 to https. hippoplayer.se (97.75.89.139) does not answer on 80 or 443. Client gap: the Modland sync index fetch (modland.c:12 ROOT https://modland.com/, modland_net.c:100) bypasses the amiga_route, so it fails with tls_unavailable. Fix: route the sync fetch through sel_mirrors_init(...,amiga_route) on SEL_NO_TLS builds, tested against the mirror fixture. The live check waits for hippoplayer.se, and the DEViLBOX base move waits on I420. Server check 2026-10-27 (both repos read): no server serves /api/mirror/modland. DEViLBOX server/src has no /api/mirror route at all. The hippoplayer.se mirror endpoint (cygnus-server, plan 2026-10-26-mirror-endpoint.md) covers only asma, sndh, keygenmusic and zxart; Modland there is a separate route, PUBLIC_PREFIX /webdata/Modules/ (cygnus-server/src/server/ModlandRoutes.ts:43), files only, no allmods index. So the client amiga_route for modland (mirrors.c:80) points at a path no server has. Decision for Johan: add modland (files and the allmods index) to the hippoplayer.se mirror endpoint, reusing ModlandFetcher, or let the Amiga fetch Modland over plain http directly (http://modland.com/ and http://ftp.modland.com/pub/modules/ answer 200). Decision (Johan, 2026-10-27): add modland, files and the allmods index, to the hippoplayer.se /api/mirror endpoint, reusing ModlandFetcher (keeps D9: one route for every Amiga source). Work order: (1) hippoplayer.se: modland mirror source plus index route, with tests, on feat/mirror; (2) MusicPlayer client: the Modland sync index fetch goes through sel_mirrors_init(..., amiga_route) on SEL_NO_TLS builds, host test against the mirror fixture; (3) live check on rig 3 once hippoplayer.se is up and the DEViLBOX base moves to it (I420). Step (2) done on main e60290c: new sel_net_amiga_route() (1 on SEL_NO_TLS, or SELECTA_AMIGA_ROUTE=1 on host); fetch.c, sync_services.c and modland_net.c passed amiga_route 0 hard-coded, now pass it; the Modland sync root comes from sel_mirrors_init("modland", 1). Test source-modland-amiga-route, revert-checked; Amiga target build/amiga compiles (rc 0). Server contract for step (1), plain http under <devilbox>: GET /api/mirror/modland/allmods.zip 200 zip with allmods.txt; GET /api/mirror/modland/pub/changelogs/changelog_YYYY-MM-DD.txt 200 or 404 (14 days asked, other statuses abort); GET /api/mirror/modland/pub/modules/<format>/<artist>/<file> module bytes. Step (1) done in hippoplayer.se feat/mirror bb5f8e0 (worktree ~/Code/hippoplayer-mirror, not pushed, not deployed): modland in mirrorSources.ts; allmods.zip and changelogs through the mirror CachedFetcher (upstream 404 stays 404); pub/modules/... served from the Modules stub tree via ensureFilled and X-Accel-Redirect /_modbytes/ (same cache as /webdata/Modules/); daily syncModland refreshes allmods.zip; nginx port 80 /api/mirror/ already generic. 3 route tests + 1 sync test, type-check clean. Unverified: the production Modules stub tree covers every allmods path (no stub = 404); nginx -t not run. | I556 | [ ] | I389, I368 | koobo (Discord, relayed by Johan 2026-10-27): "Overall a full screen update is pretty heavy so that will be a bit sluggish on aga". Johan: make screen drawing smarter, update only what changed. State today: dirty rectangles already exist (desktop_ui.c region dirty bits, rects handed to the backend), and backend_aga.c converts only dirty bands. Gaps: (1) an AGA band is always the full screen width (c2p1x1_8_c5_040 converts whole rows), so a small dirty rect still costs whole rows; (2) the double buffer unions the previous frame's bands (desk_aga_bands_union), so each change is converted twice; (3) the main cost measured on rig 2 is paint, not upload (I389: full repaint paint_ms 2170-6251, present_ms 1-2), and page changes dirty the whole screen (I368: page_dirty_pixels 480000 of 480000). Work: finish I389 to name the top paint cost; cut what page changes and hover repaint (only the regions whose content changed); measure c2p per band on AGA rig 3 before changing band width (column-limited c2p needs 32-pixel aligned spans); look at copying unchanged bands from the front buffer instead of re-running c2p for the previous frame's bands. | Rig 3 AGA --stats: paint_ms, upload pixels and frame_ms for hover, scroll and page change, before and after, in the I377 research doc; each cut has a test on dirty_pixels. | - |

Two bugs: (1) sync of the ZX source fails; (2) after a failed sync the Sync buttons vanish, so the UI is left without a way to retry. Check RAM:err.log and RAM:sel-stats on rig 2 before stopping it. Queued. Log (scratchpad i582-err.log, rig 2): one Sync click logged "error sync_failed" for ALL seven sources within about 1.3 s (modland, hippoplayer.se, asma, hvsc, keygenmusic, sndh, zxart), so the failure is not ZX-specific; the fast fail points at network/TLS setup, not the sources. Same log ends with "amiga_start: stack overflow, canary at the stack bottom overwritten (stack too small)": that run was started with run >NIL: and no Stack 262144, so the default stack overflowed at some point; recheck with the go.sh stack. Johan 2026-10-27: rig 2 has no internet, so every source failing sync in 1.3 s is expected. The remaining bug: the Sync buttons vanish after a failed sync and do not come back. Cause (2026-10-28): banner Sync click calls nag_dismiss (desktop_ui.c ~5168), which stores the global library.sync_notice_at at click time, so the engine marks every source not due for 30 days before any outcome; a failed sync never sets the sync time but stays hidden by the notice. Sources page per-source Sync buttons are not affected (drawn while remote+enabled). Decision (Johan 2026-10-28, "Stay until outcome"): Sync click stores no global notice for ticked rows; banner stays while its syncs run, rows show Syncing / Failed, a failed row keeps its checkbox and Sync retries it, succeeded rows drop out (sync time set); unticked rows are snoozed per source (new per-source notice pref, engine side in sel_source_sync_due input). Not now stays global. Regression test: failed banner sync leaves the row due and the Sync button in the dump. | 2026-10-28: part 2 done (banner per-row Syncing/Failed, failed rows retry, unticked rows snoozed per source via library.sync_snoozed "id:days" carried as SRC_SNOOZED in SOURCES; dui-sync-banner run C, revert-checked). Part 1: Johan says the mirror server is down until 2026-10-29, which explains every source failing in about 1 s (Amiga build is SEL_NO_TLS and goes through the DEViLBOX mirror proxy); bsdsocket is already on in every rig. Re-test sync once the server is up.
